# Cache factorized KEEP-delta offline gate

## A. Dataset and execution guard

- Dataset schema valid: `True`
- Candidate alignment: `PASS`
- Full supervision slots: `(1, 3, 5, 7)` in Experiment B; Experiment A uses all `(1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8)`.
- Anchor-only slots: `(2, 4, 6, 8)`.
- Additional environment / Matching / KKT / PPO executions: `0 / 0 / 0 / 0`.

## B. Factorized architecture

`Q_hat = B_KEEP(context, node) + Delta_Q(context, node, candidate, UPDATE)`; KEEP delta is structurally zero and the baseline head never receives candidate features.
